INSERT INTO webshops (
  user_id,
  title,
  locked,
  keep_money,
  partner_id,
  location_region,
  location_city,
  tariff_scale_id,
  contract_type,
  days_store_orders,
  need_courier,
  freez_return_days,
  max_order_weight,
  max_order_place_weight,
  max_order_place_length
)
SELECT
  src.user_id,
  d.title,
  0,
  src.keep_money,
  30,
  51,
  304,
  src.tariff_scale_id,
  src.contract_type,
  src.days_store_orders,
  0,
  0,
  src.max_order_weight,
  src.max_order_place_weight,
  src.max_order_place_length
FROM (
  SELECT
    'QA-Журнал: основной доступен' AS title
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'QA-Журнал: основной заблокирован'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'QA-Журнал: основного нет'
) AS d
JOIN webshops AS src
  ON src.id = 1952
LEFT JOIN webshops AS existing
  ON existing.title = d.title AND existing.partner_id = 30
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL;

UPDATE warehouses AS w
  JOIN webshops AS ws
    ON ws.id = w.member_id SET w.code = CASE w.code
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991961'
  THEN '991961'
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991962'
  THEN '991962'
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991963'
  THEN '991963'
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991964'
  THEN '991964'
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991965'
  THEN '991965'
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991966'
  THEN '991966'
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991967'
  THEN '991967'
  WHEN 'QA-JR-W991968'
  THEN '991968'
  ELSE w.code
END
WHERE
  w.member_type = 'WEBSHOP'
  AND ws.partner_id = 30
  AND ws.title IN (
    'QA-Журнал: основной доступен',
    'QA-Журнал: основной заблокирован',
    'QA-Журнал: основного нет'
  )
  AND w.code IN (
    'QA-JR-W991961',
    'QA-JR-W991962',
    'QA-JR-W991963',
    'QA-JR-W991964',
    'QA-JR-W991965',
    'QA-JR-W991966',
    'QA-JR-W991967',
    'QA-JR-W991968'
  );

INSERT INTO warehouses (
  member_type,
  member_id,
  name,
  code,
  region_id,
  city_id,
  address,
  house,
  worktime,
  is_main,
  locked
)
SELECT
  d.member_type,
  CASE WHEN d.member_id_key IS NULL THEN d.member_id ELSE ref_member_id.id END,
  d.name,
  d.code,
  d.region_id,
  d.city_id,
  d.address,
  d.house,
  d.worktime,
  d.is_main,
  d.locked
FROM (
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP' AS member_type,
    NULL AS member_id,
    'QA-Журнал: Основной доступен' AS name,
    '991961' AS code,
    51 AS region_id,
    304 AS city_id,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991961' AS address,
    '1' AS house,
    '09:00-18:00' AS worktime,
    1 AS is_main,
    0 AS locked,
    'QA-Журнал: основной доступен' AS member_id_key
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: возврат доступен',
    '991962',
    51,
    304,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991962',
    '1',
    '09:00-18:00',
    0,
    0,
    'QA-Журнал: основной доступен'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: возврат заблокирован',
    '991963',
    51,
    304,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991963',
    '1',
    '09:00-18:00',
    0,
    1,
    'QA-Журнал: основной доступен'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: Основной заблокирован',
    '991964',
    51,
    304,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991964',
    '1',
    '09:00-18:00',
    1,
    1,
    'QA-Журнал: основной заблокирован'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: возврат доступен',
    '991965',
    51,
    304,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991965',
    '1',
    '09:00-18:00',
    0,
    0,
    'QA-Журнал: основной заблокирован'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: возврат заблокирован',
    '991966',
    51,
    304,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991966',
    '1',
    '09:00-18:00',
    0,
    1,
    'QA-Журнал: основной заблокирован'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: Возврат доступен, основного нет',
    '991967',
    51,
    304,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991967',
    '1',
    '09:00-18:00',
    0,
    0,
    'QA-Журнал: основного нет'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    'WEBSHOP',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: Возврат заблокирован, основного нет',
    '991968',
    51,
    304,
    'Тестовый адрес склада возврата 991968',
    '1',
    '09:00-18:00',
    0,
    1,
    'QA-Журнал: основного нет'
) AS d
LEFT JOIN webshops AS ref_member_id
  ON ref_member_id.title = d.member_id_key AND ref_member_id.partner_id = 30
LEFT JOIN warehouses AS existing
  ON existing.member_type <=> d.member_type
  AND existing.member_id <=> CASE WHEN d.member_id_key IS NULL THEN d.member_id ELSE ref_member_id.id END
  AND existing.code <=> d.code
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL;

INSERT INTO partner_storage_areas (
  partner_id,
  warehouse_id,
  bar_code,
  title,
  locked,
  hub_id
)
SELECT
  d.partner_id,
  d.warehouse_id,
  d.bar_code,
  d.title,
  d.locked,
  d.hub_id
FROM (
  SELECT
    97 AS partner_id,
    320 AS warehouse_id,
    'QA-COURIER-93900001' AS bar_code,
    'QA-Курьерка' AS title,
    0 AS locked,
    1 AS hub_id
) AS d
LEFT JOIN partner_storage_areas AS existing
  ON existing.partner_id <=> d.partner_id
  AND existing.warehouse_id <=> d.warehouse_id
  AND existing.hub_id <=> d.hub_id
  AND existing.bar_code <=> d.bar_code
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL;

UPDATE couriers AS c
  JOIN partner_storage_areas AS a
    ON a.bar_code = 'QA-COURIER-93900001'
    AND a.partner_id = c.partner_id
    AND a.warehouse_id = 320
    AND a.hub_id = c.hub_id SET c.area_id = a.id
WHERE
  c.id = 16 AND c.partner_id = 97 AND c.hub_id = 1 AND c.locked = 0;

INSERT INTO shipments (
  transport_doc_number,
  type,
  places,
  weight,
  need_intake,
  sender_id,
  receiver_id,
  delivery_region_id,
  delivery_city_id,
  date_create,
  status_id,
  status,
  sender_warehouse_id,
  receiver_warehouse_id
)
SELECT
  d.transport_doc_number,
  d.type,
  d.places,
  d.weight,
  d.need_intake,
  d.sender_id,
  d.receiver_id,
  d.delivery_region_id,
  d.delivery_city_id,
  NOW(),
  (
    SELECT
      id
    FROM shipment_statuses_new
    WHERE
      name = d.status

  d.status,
  d.sender_warehouse_id,
  d.receiver_warehouse_id
FROM (
  SELECT
    'QA-IM-1-1' AS transport_doc_number,
    'FROM_WEBSHOP' AS type,

    0 AS need_intake,

INSERT INTO orders (
  webshop_id,
  partner_webshop_id,
  partner_executor_id,
  region_delivery_id,
  region_webshop_id,
  city_delivery_id,
  city_webshop_id,
  date_create,
  webshop_number,
  bar_code,
  status_id,
  work_status_id,
  problem_status_id,
  quantity_places,
  return_warehouse_id,
  delivery_street,
  current_cost,
  client_cost,
  current_owner_type,
  supplier_id,
  current_partner_id,
  current_shipment_id,
  ws_desire_date_delivery
)
SELECT
  CASE WHEN d.webshop_id_key IS NULL THEN d.webshop_id ELSE ref_webshop_id.id END,
  d.partner_webshop_id,
  d.partner_executor_id,
  d.region_delivery_id,
  d.region_webshop_id,
  d.city_delivery_id,
  d.city_webshop_id,
  NOW(),
  d.webshop_number,
  CASE
    WHEN d.webshop_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.bar_code
    ELSE CONCAT(ref_webshop_id.id, SUBSTRING(d.bar_code, LOCATE('*', d.bar_code)))
  END,
  d.status_id,
  d.work_status_id,
  d.problem_status_id,
  d.quantity_places,
  CASE
    WHEN d.return_warehouse_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.return_warehouse_id
    ELSE ref_return_warehouse_id.id
  END,
  d.delivery_street,
  d.current_cost,
  d.client_cost,
  d.current_owner_type,
  d.supplier_id,
  d.current_partner_id,
  CASE
    WHEN d.current_shipment_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.current_shipment_id
    ELSE ref_current_shipment_id.id
  END,
  CASE
    WHEN d.use_work_date = 1
    THEN CASE
      WHEN DATE_FORMAT(NOW(), '%H%i') > '2030'
      THEN DATE_ADD(CURRENT_DATE, INTERVAL '1' DAY)
      ELSE CURRENT_DATE
    END
    ELSE NULL
  END
FROM (
  SELECT
    1952 AS webshop_id,
    30 AS partner_webshop_id,
    97 AS partner_executor_id,
    51 AS region_delivery_id,
    51 AS region_webshop_id,
    304 AS city_delivery_id,
    304 AS city_webshop_id,
    'QA-01-01' AS webshop_number,
    '1952*QA-01-01' AS bar_code,
    1 AS status_id,
    1 AS work_status_id,
    0 AS problem_status_id,
    1 AS quantity_places,
    339 AS return_warehouse_id,
    'Тестовая улица' AS delivery_street,
    0.0 AS current_cost,
    0.0 AS client_cost,
    'WEBSHOP' AS current_owner_type,
    0 AS supplier_id,
    0 AS current_partner_id,
    NULL AS current_shipment_id,
    0 AS use_work_date,
    NULL AS webshop_id_key,
    NULL AS return_warehouse_id_key,
    'QA-IM-1-1' AS current_shipment_id_key
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    1952,
    30,
    97,
    51,
    51,
    304,
    304,
    'QA-01-01-2-1',
    '1952*QA-01-01-2-1',
    1,

  UNION ALL
  SELECT

) AS d
LEFT JOIN webshops AS ref_webshop_id
  ON ref_webshop_id.title = d.webshop_id_key AND ref_webshop_id.partner_id = 30
LEFT JOIN warehouses AS ref_return_warehouse_id
  ON ref_return_warehouse_id.code = d.return_warehouse_id_key
  AND (
    ref_return_warehouse_id.member_type = 'WEBSHOP'
    AND ref_return_warehouse_id.member_id = ref_webshop_id.id
  )
LEFT JOIN shipments AS ref_current_shipment_id
  ON ref_current_shipment_id.transport_doc_number = d.current_shipment_id_key
WHERE
  1 = 1 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE webshop_id = VALUES(webshop_id), partner_webshop_id = VALUES(partner_webshop_id), partner_executor_id = VALUES(partner_executor_id), region_delivery_id = VALUES(region_delivery_id), region_webshop_id = VALUES(region_webshop_id), city_delivery_id = VALUES(city_delivery_id), city_webshop_id = VALUES(city_webshop_id), date_create = VALUES(date_create), webshop_number = VALUES(webshop_number), bar_code = VALUES(bar_code), status_id = VALUES(status_id), work_status_id = VALUES(work_status_id), problem_status_id = VALUES(problem_status_id), quantity_places = VALUES(quantity_places), return_warehouse_id = VALUES(return_warehouse_id), delivery_street = VALUES(delivery_street), current_cost = VALUES(current_cost), client_cost = VALUES(client_cost), current_owner_type = VALUES(current_owner_type), supplier_id = VALUES(supplier_id), current_partner_id = VALUES(current_partner_id), current_shipment_id = VALUES(current_shipment_id), ws_desire_date_delivery = VALUES(ws_desire_date_delivery);

INSERT INTO order_places (
  order_id,
  index_number,
  bar_code,
  status_id,
  work_status_id,
  problem_status_id,
  date_problem_status,
  date_create,
  declared_price,
  current_shipment_id,
  current_owner_type,
  current_partner_id,
  warehouse_id,
  wms_problem_id,
  webshop_barcode
)
SELECT
  CASE WHEN d.order_id_key IS NULL THEN d.order_id ELSE ref_order_id.id END,
  d.index_number,
  CONCAT(
    SUBSTRING_INDEX(ref_order_id.bar_code, '*', 1),
    SUBSTRING(d.bar_code, LOCATE('*', d.bar_code))
  ),
  d.status_id,
  d.work_status_id,
  d.problem_status_id,
  d.date_problem_status,
  NOW(),
  d.declared_price,
  CASE
    WHEN d.current_shipment_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.current_shipment_id
    ELSE ref_current_shipment_id.id
  END,
  d.current_owner_type,
  d.current_partner_id,
  d.warehouse_id,
  d.wms_problem_id,
  d.webshop_barcode
FROM (
  SELECT
    NULL AS order_id,
    1 AS index_number,
    '1952*QA-01-01' AS bar_code,
    1 AS status_id,
    1 AS work_status_id,
    0 AS problem_status_id,
    NULL AS date_problem_status,
    0.0 AS declared_price,
    NULL AS current_shipment_id,

    'QA-Журнал: основного нет'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    1,
    '991953*QA-JR-N10-2',
    8,
    1,
    0,
    NULL,
    0.0,
    0,
    'PARTNER_WEBSHOP',
    30,
    319,
    2,
    NULL,
    '*QA-JR-N10-2',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: основного нет'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    1,
    '991953*QA-JR-N10-3',
    8,
    1,
    0,
    NULL,
    0.0,
    0,
    'PARTNER_WEBSHOP',
    30,
    319,
    2,
    NULL,
    '*QA-JR-N10-3',
    NULL,
    'QA-Журнал: основного нет'
) AS d
LEFT JOIN webshops AS ref_order_webshop
  ON ref_order_webshop.title = d.order_webshop_title_key
  AND ref_order_webshop.partner_id = 30
LEFT JOIN orders AS ref_order_id
  ON ref_order_id.bar_code = CASE
    WHEN d.order_webshop_title_key IS NULL
    THEN d.order_id_key
    ELSE CONCAT(ref_order_webshop.id, d.order_id_key)
  END
LEFT JOIN shipments AS ref_current_shipment_id
  ON ref_current_shipment_id.transport_doc_number = d.current_shipment_id_key
WHERE
  1 = 1 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE order_id = VALUES(order_id), index_number = VALUES(index_number), bar_code = VALUES(bar_code), status_id = VALUES(status_id), work_status_id = VALUES(work_status_id), problem_status_id = VALUES(problem_status_id), date_problem_status = VALUES(date_problem_status), date_create = VALUES(date_create), declared_price = VALUES(declared_price), current_shipment_id = VALUES(current_shipment_id), current_owner_type = VALUES(current_owner_type), current_partner_id = VALUES(current_partner_id), warehouse_id = VALUES(warehouse_id), wms_problem_id = VALUES(wms_problem_id), webshop_barcode = VALUES(webshop_barcode);

  shipment_id,
  bar_code,
  number,
  weight,
  pallets
)

  d.number,
  d.weight,
  1

LEFT JOIN shipments AS ref_shipment_id
  ON ref_shipment_id.transport_doc_number = d.shipment_id_key
LEFT JOIN shipment_seals AS existing
  ON existing.shipment_id <=> CASE WHEN d.shipment_id_key IS NULL THEN d.shipment_id ELSE ref_shipment_id.id END
  AND existing.number <=> d.number
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL;

INSERT INTO shipments_orders (
  shipment_id,
  order_id
)

  CASE WHEN d.shipment_id_key IS NULL THEN d.shipment_id ELSE ref_shipment_id.id END,

) AS d
LEFT JOIN shipments AS ref_shipment_id
  ON ref_shipment_id.transport_doc_number = d.shipment_id_key
LEFT JOIN orders AS ref_order_id
  ON ref_order_id.bar_code = d.order_id_key
LEFT JOIN shipments_orders AS existing
  ON existing.shipment_id <=> CASE WHEN d.shipment_id_key IS NULL THEN d.shipment_id ELSE ref_shipment_id.id END
  AND existing.order_id <=> CASE WHEN d.order_id_key IS NULL THEN d.order_id ELSE ref_order_id.id END
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL;

INSERT INTO shipment_order_places (
  order_id,
  order_place_id,
  shipment_id
)
SELECT
  CASE WHEN d.order_id_key IS NULL THEN d.order_id ELSE ref_order_id.id END,
  CASE
    WHEN d.order_place_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.order_place_id
    ELSE ref_order_place_id.id
  END,
  CASE WHEN d.shipment_id_key IS NULL THEN d.shipment_id ELSE ref_shipment_id.id END
FROM (
  SELECT
    NULL AS order_id,
    NULL AS order_place_id,
    NULL AS shipment_id,
    'QA-IM-1-1' AS shipment_id_key,
    '1952*QA-01-01' AS order_id_key,
    '1952*QA-01-01' AS order_place_id_key
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'QA-IM-1-2',
    '1952*QA-01-01-2-1',
    '1952*QA-01-01-2-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'QA-IM-1-3',
    '1952*QA-01-01-3-1',
    '1952*QA-01-01-3-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'QA-IM-2-1',
    '1952*QA-01-02',
    '1952*QA-01-02'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'QA-IM-2-2',
    '1952*QA-01-02-2-1',
    '1952*QA-01-02-2-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'QA-IM-2-3',
    '1952*QA-01-02-3-1',
    '1952*QA-01-02-3-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    NULL,

    NULL,

    '1952*QA-08-03-3-1',
    '1952*QA-08-03-3-1'
) AS d
LEFT JOIN shipments AS ref_shipment_id
  ON ref_shipment_id.transport_doc_number = d.shipment_id_key
LEFT JOIN orders AS ref_order_id
  ON ref_order_id.bar_code = d.order_id_key
LEFT JOIN order_places AS ref_order_place_id
  ON ref_order_place_id.bar_code = d.order_place_id_key
LEFT JOIN shipment_order_places AS existing
  ON existing.shipment_id <=> CASE WHEN d.shipment_id_key IS NULL THEN d.shipment_id ELSE ref_shipment_id.id END
  AND existing.order_place_id <=> CASE
    WHEN d.order_place_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.order_place_id
    ELSE ref_order_place_id.id
  END
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL;

INSERT INTO kafka_places (
  shipment_id,
  order_place_id,
  event_type,
  current_warehouse_id,
  next_warehouse_id,
  created_at
)
SELECT
  CASE WHEN d.shipment_id_key IS NULL THEN d.shipment_id ELSE ref_shipment_id.id END,
  CASE
    WHEN d.order_place_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.order_place_id
    ELSE ref_order_place_id.id
  END,
  d.event_type,
  d.current_warehouse_id,
  d.next_warehouse_id,

    'SHIPMENTS_INCOME',
    320,
    319,
    0,
    'TD-QA-06-11-3-1',
    '1952*QA-06-11-3-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'SHIPMENTS_INCOME',
    320,
    319,
    0,
    'TD-QA-06-12-1-1',
    '1952*QA-06-12-1-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'SHIPMENTS_INCOME',
    320,
    319,
    0,
    'TD-QA-06-12-2-1',
    '1952*QA-06-12-2-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'SHIPMENTS_INCOME',
    320,
    319,
    0,
    'TD-QA-06-12-3-1',
    '1952*QA-06-12-3-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'SHIPMENTS_INCOME',
    320,
    320,
    0,
    'TD-QA-06-06-1-1',
    '1952*QA-06-05'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'SHIPMENTS_INCOME',
    320,
    320,
    0,
    'TD-QA-06-06-2-1',
    '1952*QA-06-06-2-1'
  UNION ALL
  SELECT
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'SHIPMENTS_INCOME',
    320,
    320,
    0,
    'TD-QA-06-06-3-1',
    '1952*QA-06-06-3-1'
) AS d
LEFT JOIN shipments AS ref_shipment_id
  ON ref_shipment_id.transport_doc_number = d.shipment_id_key
LEFT JOIN order_places AS ref_order_place_id
  ON ref_order_place_id.bar_code = d.order_place_id_key
LEFT JOIN kafka_places AS existing
  ON existing.shipment_id <=> CASE WHEN d.shipment_id_key IS NULL THEN d.shipment_id ELSE ref_shipment_id.id END
  AND existing.order_place_id <=> CASE
    WHEN d.order_place_id_key IS NULL
    THEN d.order_place_id
    ELSE ref_order_place_id.id
  END
  AND existing.event_type <=> d.event_type
  AND existing.current_warehouse_id <=> d.current_warehouse_id
  AND existing.next_warehouse_id <=> d.next_warehouse_id
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL;

INSERT INTO courier_orders (
  courier_id,
  order_id,
  status_id,
  created_at,
  order_date_delivery
)
SELECT
  d.courier_id,
  CASE WHEN d.order_id_key IS NULL THEN d.order_id ELSE ref_order_id.id END,
  d.status_id,
  DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL d.created_minutes_ago MINUTE),
  DATE_ADD(
    CASE
      WHEN DATE_FORMAT(NOW(), '%H%i') > '2030'
      THEN DATE_ADD(CURRENT_DATE, INTERVAL '1' DAY)
      ELSE CURRENT_DATE
    END,
    INTERVAL d.delivery_days_offset DAY
  )
FROM (
  SELECT
    16 AS courier_id,
    NULL AS order_id,
    1 AS status_id,

LEFT JOIN orders AS ref_order_id
  ON ref_order_id.bar_code = d.order_id_key
LEFT JOIN courier_orders AS existing
  ON existing.courier_id <=> d.courier_id
  AND existing.order_id <=> CASE WHEN d.order_id_key IS NULL THEN d.order_id ELSE ref_order_id.id END
  AND existing.order_date_delivery <=> DATE_ADD(
    CASE
      WHEN DATE_FORMAT(NOW(), '%H%i') > '2030'
      THEN DATE_ADD(CURRENT_DATE, INTERVAL '1' DAY)
      ELSE CURRENT_DATE
    END,
    INTERVAL d.delivery_days_offset DAY
  )
  AND existing.status_id <=> d.status_id
WHERE
  existing.id IS NULL
ORDER BY
  DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL d.created_minutes_ago MINUTE),
  d.status_id DESC;
